Today, analysis of the future of the world's population is being conducted around the world, especially in Europe, with an elderly population, with emphasis on the importance of the issue. According to population estimates of 2050, the world's population will exceed 9 billion. Our Turkey, which was estimated to have a population of 94,585 thousand people in 2050, is expected to rank 19th among 187 countries. According to 2012 data, the world's population growth rate is 1.2 per cent, and the number is 1.25 per cent in Turkey. The rate of population growth around the world is dwindling day by day, and with the entire world population, unfortunately, Turkey's population is growing. I'm sorry to say that if we don't do the necessary work, it's inevitable that Turkey will face this problem in the future. Turkey is expected to reduce its population rate to 2/1,000 during the period 2045-2050. In this case, Turkey's rate of population growth is expected to drop below the world population growth rate, which is estimated to be 4 per thousand for the same period. The aging of the population is the changing of the demographic structure. The aging of the population has economic and social destructive effects. With their population aging, it is inevitable that countries will suffer from an active labour force. With the rate of population growth dwindling, the working population will continue to decline and Turkey's economy will be in danger of collapse. The countries that can continue to grow by maintaining the rate of population growth will be among the powerful states of the future. For our country, which is part of the world's first 20 economies, it is vital to our future that we maintain the rate of population growth. Aware of the seriousness of the issue, the United Nations has identified one of the three global warfare areas as population aging, along with global warming and global terrorism. There is absolutely no question that we, the Population Development Group of Turkey, are indifferent to the aging of the population. The problem is very serious, and the problem should be dealt with frequently. Based on the fact that the population is a force in world politics, our country has never had the luxury of being weak in this regard. Today, nine of every child under the age of 15 lives in developing countries. These countries are expected to continue to be the highest birthing countries. In light of these analysis, 70 percent of the growth in the world's population will take place in the developing 24 countries by 2050, and their population is expected to be one of the most important forces in these countries. In this minival, the reduction in population growth must be halted and reversible for a few generations of time planned and encouraged by reproduction, or the country must receive foreign migration. In addition, the fertility rate per family must be 2.1 so that a nation can sustain it for more than 25 years, at least the population remains stable and does not grow old. The proposal our Prime Minister made to the families to have three children must be reconsidered within the framework of all these analyses and statistics. According to the scientific data in Turkey, it is possible to keep the population at today's youth rates, perhaps with the minimum of three children in each family. Therefore, you valued MPs and my call to our entire nation is to listen to our Prime Minister's words. Turkey to the chairman of the Grand National Assembly In order to protect Lake Van in its original form, to contribute more to country and world science, to be an important stop in tourism by promoting it, to be able to contribute more to the country's economy, and to determine what needs to be done, we provide and offer the constitution's requirements for the opening of parliamentary research under Articles 104, Internal Charter 105. [[Bitlis]] [[Lug]] [[Şırnak]] [[Mwah.]] [[Adana]] [[Pain]] 7) Ayla Akat Ata [[Batman]] [[Bingöl]] [[Diyarbakır]] [[Diyarbakır]] [[Diyarbakır]] [[Hakkari]] [[Hakkari]] 14) The Leader of the Secret [[İstanbul]] [[İstanbul]] [[Kars]] [[Mardin]] [[Mersin]] [[Mwah.]] [[Şanlıurfa]] [[Van]] [[Van]] [[Diyarbakır]] Lake Van is an original ecosystem that is unique in the world with salt and soda water (pH 9.8) in a thousand). This originality draws the attention of scientists all over the world to Lake Van. With this structure, Lake Van is studied by paleolimnologists with great interest in enlightening the formation of young oceans. The lake, on the other hand, is considered a sign of water level changes and global climate changes. In 2003, EU FP-6 will monitor the indicators of global climate change around the world. As part of the Framework Programme, the project "Ecowater" was designed to be the lake where the ecological effects of the Van Lake climate change were best monitored, and the model of the project was designed to be considered Lake Van Lake. So Lake Van isn't just a tiny, insignificant lake to experiment on that would upset all the ecological balances of the lake. It's an original ecosystem that has its present structure and the attention of the world. Pearl kephali is also a species of fish found only in Lake Van in the world. Pearl kefali is also a major source of income for the people of the region. The 2010 figures and its contribution to the country's economy are $10m. Fourteen thousand people around the lake make a living producing pearl kephali. There are more than 60 internationally important wetlands in our country. Three of these wetlands, including Ramsar, CITES, Bern, are located in the vicinity of Lake Van Lake Van (Bendi Mahi Sazı, Çelebagek Sazı, Lake Arn), which our country is part of, according to international contract provisions. Around 300 species of birds have been detected in these wetlands so far. The change in the lake ecosystem will negatively affect these wetlands and the biodiversity that lives in wetlands. For all these reasons, Lake Van is one of the most important natural riches Turkey has to protect carefully. The implementation of any project that could disrupt the structure of Lake Van means the breakdown of an original ecosystem, the center of attention in the world. Because of the negative impact of the lake's tamponing effect, it means that salt and soda water can't be transformed into fresh water. That means the reproduction and migration of the endemic pearl kephalin, which only lives in the Lake Van basin and is included in the red list by the IUCN. It will also mean damage to the internationally important wetlands and biodiversity around the lake. It is very important that the Lake Van can be protected in its original form, continue to contribute to country and world science, promote it, be a major stop in tourism, and open a parliamentary study by the TBMM to determine what needs to be done. tbmm-2013-07-11sit02spe0119par0189	Mr. President, I have made a pledge on behalf of my own person on the motion that those appointed as ambassadors and permanent representatives of the State Department, when they return to our country, will have the same rights as those who are members of the profession. In accordance with Article 12 (d) of Law No. 6004, those appointed as ambassadors under Article 557 of the Law continue to work at the Ministry and to use the title ambassador for their services when they return to our country. Those who do not need their services are cut off from the Ministry and extradited to their former public institutions and institutions. Likewise, those appointed as permanent representatives are subject to the same provisions. These diplomats, appointed as ambassadors and permanent representatives, are a representative of our state in another country and are tasked to conduct relations between its country and its country. To conduct diplomatic relations between the government of the country and its government, to conduct political systems, to conduct research on international law, political parties, to interpret the results of these research, and to make suggestions on developing political behavior; to represent our country in the best way according to its mandate; to care for the problems of citizens in that country, to secure visa agreements with countries on visas. The law currently under way and the ambassador and our permanent representatives, who carry such important tasks and responsibilities, are not entitled to the same rights as ambassadors and permanent representatives who are members of the ministry when they return to our homeland. With this motion, we intended to address the victims of ambassadors and permanent representatives who were appointed without the State Department. When the ambassador and permanent representatives who are not members of the State Department will be able to complete their term abroad and return to the country, they will be able to work within the State Department and have the same rights as the Ministry. In addition, under Article 557 of Law 59, those appointed as ambassadors and permanent representatives will be able to be appointed to the Foreign Ministry or to the heads of the branch office at the Ministry of Foreign Affairs or to the higher executive staff. In 2002, when our EC PARTY government took office, our number of foreign diplomatic missions was 163, and in 2013 the number rose to 219. In addition, we have 93 embassies and 58 consulates in 2002, while as of 2013, we have 129 embassies and 78 consulates. In the period of our EC PARTY administrations, 11 ambassadors were appointed under Article 59 of Law 657, not as an official of the State Department. As of today, 10 ambassadors from outside the ministry are serving abroad, one ambassador has completed his term and returned to headquarters. All appointed ambassadors have public history and advanced foreign grammar. In general, appointments were made between senior bureaucrats or university professors. With these new appointments, our country's number of embassies and permanent diplomatic missions abroad will be 140 and the number of foreign ambassadors will be 12.
